Maths required.” }, ]; // ════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ALL_PROGRAMS — Combined from all sources // Total: 1012+ programmes across all Ghanaian accredited institutions // ════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── ALL PROGRAMMES ── const ALL_PROGRAMS = [ // Base explicit programmes from UNIVERSITIES array …UNIVERSITIES.flatMap(u => u.programmes.map((p, idx) => ({ …p, id: u.code + “_” + idx, uniCode: u.code, uniName: u.name, uniType: u.type, uniLoc: u.loc })) ), // Generated programmes (tech unis HND, colleges of education, nursing colleges) …GENERATED_PROGRAMS, // Additional explicit programmes (private unis, health programmes, specialties) …EXTRA_PROGRAMS, // Top-up programmes (specialties, emerging fields) …TOPUP_PROGRAMS, ]; // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════ // INTELLIGENT MATCHING ENGINE // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ================================================================ // WASSCE AGGREGATE ENGINE — Official Formula // // KNUST / UG / UCC formula: // Aggregate = English + Core Maths + Integrated Science + 3 relevant electives // (Social Studies is a pass requirement, NOT counted in the aggregate) // // The 3 elective slots are filled as: // Step 1: Lock required electives for this programme (e.g. Biology for Medicine) // Step 2: Fill remaining slots from student’s other electives (best grades first) // Step 3: Sum = English + Maths + Science + those 3 electives // // Example (Medicine): Required = Biology, Chemistry, Physics // English A1(1) + Maths A1(1) + Science A1(1) + Biology B2(2) + Chem A1(1) + Phys A1(1) = 7 // // Example (Engineering): Required = Elective Maths, Physics // English A1(1) + Maths A1(1) + Science A1(1) + El.Maths C6(6) + Phys A1(1) + [best other](1) = 11 // (Note: Biology is NOT counted here even if student has A1 — not relevant to Engineering) // ================================================================ // Global best-6 aggregate (for display / quick calculator only) // Programme-specific aggregate — the accurate calculation used for matching // Formula: English + Core Maths + Integrated Science + 3 relevant electives // ── AGGREGATE ENGINE ── // ── AGGREGATE ENGINE ── const calcProgAggregate = (prog, coreGrades, electivesWithGrades) => { // ================================================================ // OFFICIAL WASSCE AGGREGATE FORMULA (programme-aware) // // Standard formula: English + Core Maths + BEST(Science OR Social) + 3 relevant electives // // The 3rd core slot (Science vs Social Studies) depends on: // 1. KNUST: uses Science for ALL programmes EXCEPT Law & Political Science // (where Social Studies is used instead) // 2. UMaT: always uses Science over Social Studies // 3. All other universities: pick the BETTER of Science or Social Studies // BUT science-based programmes prefer Science, arts/social prefer Social // // Nursing at KNUST: General Science track only // The 3 elective slots: required electives first, then best remaining // ================================================================ const engPt = GP[coreGrades.english] || null; const mthPt = GP[coreGrades.maths] || null; const sciPt = GP[coreGrades.science] || null; const socPt = GP[coreGrades.social] || null; if (!engPt || !mthPt) return null; // English and Maths always required // ── Determine which 3rd core subject to use ── const uniCode = (prog.uniCode || “”).replace(/_.*/, “”); // strip suffixes const progName = (prog.name || “”).toLowerCase(); const isKNUST = uniCode === “KNUST” || uniCode === “KNUST2”; const isUMaT = uniCode === “UMaT”; // KNUST Law & Political Science use Social Studies instead of Science const knustSocialProg = isKNUST && ( progName.includes(“law”) || progName.includes(“political science”) || progName.includes(“communication & media”) || progName.includes(“geography”) || progName.includes(“history”) || progName.includes(“sociology”) || progName.includes(“french”) || progName.includes(“english language”) ); let thirdCorePt; if (isKNUST && !knustSocialProg) { // KNUST: always uses Science (not Social) for science/engineering/business/health thirdCorePt = sciPt; } else if (isKNUST && knustSocialProg) { // KNUST Law, Political Science etc: uses Social Studies thirdCorePt = socPt; } else if (isUMaT) { // UMaT: always Science over Social thirdCorePt = sciPt; } else { // All other universities: pick the BETTER (lower number = better) of Science/Social // But also consider what the programme requires: // Science-based tracks prefer Science; Arts/Social/Business prefer Social const sciTrack = prog.tracks && ( prog.tracks.includes(“General Science”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Technical”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Agricultural Science”) ); const artsTrack = prog.tracks && ( prog.tracks.includes(“General Arts”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Business”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Home Economics”) || prog.tracks.includes(“Visual Arts”) ); ``` if (sciTrack && !artsTrack) { // Science programme — use Science if available, fall back to Social thirdCorePt = sciPt || socPt; } else if (artsTrack && !sciTrack) { // Arts/Social programme — use Social if available, fall back to Science thirdCorePt = socPt || sciPt; } else { // Mixed or open tracks — pick whichever is better (lower = better grade) if (sciPt && socPt) { thirdCorePt = Math.min(sciPt, socPt); } else { thirdCorePt = sciPt || socPt; } } ``` } if (!thirdCorePt) return null; // need at least 3 cores const corePts = [engPt, mthPt, thirdCorePt]; // exactly 3 cores // ── Required electives for this programme ── const required = prog.requiredElectives || []; // Step 1: Lock required electives the student has const requiredPts = []; for (const req of required) { const found = electivesWithGrades.find(e => e.subject === req && e.grade); if (found) requiredPts.push(GP[found.grade] || 9); // If missing required elective, subject check will flag ineligible } // Step 2: Fill remaining slots from student’s OTHER electives (best grades first) const slotsNeeded = 3 - requiredPts.length; const optionalPts = electivesWithGrades .filter(e => !required.includes(e.subject) && e.grade && (GP[e.grade] || 9) <= 8) .map(e => GP[e.grade]) .sort((a, b) => a - b) .slice(0, Math.max(0, slotsNeeded)); // Step 3: Total = 3 cores + up to 3 electives const all = […corePts, …requiredPts, …optionalPts]; if (all.length < 4) return null; return all.reduce((s, p) => s + p, 0); }; // ── SUBJECT REQUIREMENTS CHECKER ── // ── SUBJECT CHECKER ── const checkSubjectRequirements = (prog, studentTrack, studentElectives, studentCoreGrades, electivesWithGrades) => { const issues = []; const passes = []; const uniCode = (prog.uniCode || “”).replace(/_.*/, “”); const progName = (prog.name || “”).toLowerCase(); // ── 1. TRACK CHECK ── if (prog.tracks && prog.tracks.length > 0) { if (!prog.tracks.includes(studentTrack)) { issues.push(“Requires “ + (prog.tracks.join(” or “)) + “ track (you are on “ + (studentTrack) + “ track)”); } else { passes.push(“✓ Your “ + (studentTrack) + “ track qualifies”); } } // ── 2. SPECIAL KNUST NURSING / MIDWIFERY RULE ── // KNUST Nursing and Midwifery are General Science ONLY — no exceptions const isKNUST = uniCode === “KNUST” || uniCode === “KNUST2”; const isNursingOrMidwifery = progName.includes(“nursing”) || progName.includes(“midwifery”); if (isKNUST && isNursingOrMidwifery && studentTrack !== “General Science”) { issues.push(“KNUST Nursing and Midwifery are strictly for General Science students only”); } // ── 3. REQUIRED ELECTIVES CHECK ── if (prog.requiredElectives && prog.requiredElectives.length > 0) { prog.requiredElectives.forEach(req => { if (!studentElectives.includes(req)) { issues.push(“Requires “ + (req) + “ as an elective (not in your combination)”); } else { passes.push(“✓ You have “ + (req)); } }); } else { passes.push(“✓ No specific elective combination required”); } // ── 4. REQUIRED CORE SUBJECTS CHECK ── if (prog.requiredCore && prog.requiredCore.length > 0) { prog.requiredCore.forEach(req => { const coreMap = { “English”:“english”, “Maths”:“maths”, “Science”:“science”, “Social Studies”:“social” }; const key = coreMap[req]; if (key && !studentCoreGrades[key]) { issues.push(“Core “ + (req) + “ grade is required”); } else if (key) { passes.push(“✓ Core “ + (req) + “ present”); } }); } // ── 5. SCIENCE CORE REQUIREMENT ── // KNUST (non-social programmes) and UMaT require Integrated Science // If student has no science grade for these universities, flag it const isUMaT = uniCode === “UMaT”; const knustSocialProg = isKNUST && ( progName.includes(“law”) || progName.includes(“political science”) || progName.includes(“geography”) || progName.includes(“history”) || progName.includes(“sociology”) || progName.includes(“french”) || progName.includes(“english language”) || progName.includes(“communication & media”) ); if ((isKNUST && !knustSocialProg) || isUMaT) { if (!studentCoreGrades.science) { issues.push((uniCode) + “ requires Integrated Science grade for this programme”); } else { passes.push(“✓ Integrated Science grade present (required by “ + (uniCode) + “)”); } } // ── 6. MINIMUM GRADE CHECKS ── if (prog.minGrades && electivesWithGrades) { Object.entries(prog.minGrades).forEach(([subj, minGrade]) => { const coreMap = { “English”:“english”, “Maths”:“maths”, “Science”:“science”, “Social Studies”:“social” }; const coreKey = coreMap[subj]; let studentGrade = null; ``` if (coreKey) { studentGrade = studentCoreGrades[coreKey]; } else { const found = (electivesWithGrades || []).find(e => e.subject === subj); if (found) studentGrade = found.grade; } if (studentGrade) { const minPts = GP[minGrade] || 9; const stuPts = GP[studentGrade] || 9; if (stuPts > minPts) { issues.push((subj) + " requires minimum " + (minGrade) + " (you have " + (studentGrade) + ")"); } else { passes.push("✓ " + (subj) + " grade " + (studentGrade) + " meets minimum " + (minGrade)); } } }); ``` } return { eligible: issues.length === 0, issues, passes }; } const ANNS = [“2025/2026 Admissions Open — UG, KNUST, UCC, GIMPA, UPSA accepting applications”,“WASSCE Results Released — Check Your University Eligibility Now”,“Cut-Off Points Updated for 2025/2026”,“Premium Plan: Check All 42 Universities for Just GHC15”]; const NEWS = [{t:“UG 2025/2026 Admissions About

About UniPredict Ghana

Our Mission

UniPredict Ghana is Ghana’s most comprehensive university admission intelligence platform. We help WASSCE students make informed decisions about their university applications by providing accurate eligibility checks, verified cut-off points, and personalised programme recommendations.

We cover all 42 accredited Ghanaian universities with over 1,000 programmes, using the official WASSCE aggregate formula to give you precise, programme-specific eligibility results.
